- **read-before-edit**: Use before editing an unfamiliar file — locate the symbol with search, read only the surrounding code, match its style. ([rusyander/claude-control](https://github.com/rusyander/claude-control/tree/HEAD/apps/server/assets/kit/agentdeck-kit/skills/read-before-edit))
